### [1] [The Local Star Formation Environment Predicts Young Star Cluster Radii: Evidence from PHANGS-MUSE H-alpha Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23876)
*Michelle Sebastian*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文研究了年轻星团的有效半径与其质量之间的关系，并发现局部恒星形成率面密度（Sigma_SFR）比星系整体的比恒星形成率（sSFR）更能预测星团半径，表明星团结构受其诞生环境影响，且该影响仅在星团实际形成的尺度上显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理论预测星团初始半径依赖于其诞生气体环境的面密度，因此恒星形成环境应在星团结构中留下可观测印记；但此前基于星系尺度sSFR的研究未发现预测能力，作者旨在检验是否因环境测量尺度过大所致。

Method: 将LEGUS星团样本与PHANGS-MUSE H-alpha图像交叉匹配，在四个共有星系中对325个<10 Myr年轻星团进行孔径光度法测量其本地Sigma_SFR，并构建包含质量、年龄及Sigma_SFR的回归模型，对比本地与星系尺度环境变量的预测能力（含固定效应和交叉验证）。

Result: 本地Sigma_SFR显著预测星团有效半径（β=0.079, p=2×10⁻⁴），而星系sSFR不显著（β=0.042, p=0.40）；本地模型交叉验证R²达0.061（远高于0.022）；效应在单星系内稳健（固定效应下β=0.068, p=0.001），并使质量系数降低26%。

Conclusion: 星团诞生环境确实塑造其结构，但仅当环境参数在星团实际形成的局域尺度（而非星系整体尺度）上测量时才可被探测到。

Abstract: The effective radii of young star clusters correlate with cluster mass, but the large scatter about that relation is not well understood. Theory predicts that a cluster's initial radius depends on the gas surface density of its natal environment, so the star formation environment should leave a measurable imprint on cluster structure. In a companion analysis of 5,105 clusters across 31 LEGUS galaxies, we found that galaxy-wide specific star formation rate (sSFR) carries no predictive power for cluster radius. Here we test whether that null result reflects the spatial scale on which the environment was measured. We cross-match the LEGUS cluster catalog of Brown & Gnedin with H-alpha maps from the PHANGS-MUSE survey in the four galaxies common to both surveys, and measure the local star formation rate surface density, Sigma_SFR, at the position of every cluster by aperture photometry. For the 325 clusters younger than 10 Myr with reliable radius and mass measurements, local Sigma_SFR significantly predicts effective radius (beta = 0.079, p = 2 x 10^-4) in a model containing mass and age, while the host galaxy's sSFR does not (beta = 0.042, p = 0.40) for those same clusters. The local model generalizes nearly three times better under cross-validation (CV R^2 = 0.061 versus 0.022). The effect operates among clusters within a single galaxy: it survives galaxy fixed effects (beta = 0.068, p = 0.001) and appears independently in each of the two galaxies large enough to fit alone. Including local Sigma_SFR also reduces the mass coefficient by 26%. The birth environment measurably shapes cluster structure, but only when it is measured on the scale on which clusters actually form.

</details>


### [2] [Beyond the dust: JWST reveals extinction and ice content in local (U)LIRGs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24069)
*G. Speranza,M. Pereira-Santaella,I. García-Bernete,E. González-Alfonso,S. Arribas,I. Lamperti,M. Perna,A. Alonso-Herrero,L. Hermosa Muñoz,D. Rigopoulou,N. Thatte*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用JWST/NIRSpec对四个邻近相互作用的(U)LIRGs星系进行空间分辨观测，发展了三种方法分别测定电离气体和热分子气体的相特异性消光，以及H2O和CO2冰柱密度；发现核区比延展区具有更高的消光和冰柱密度，电离气体比热分子气体更致密，而水冰则包裹两种气体相，且CO2/H2O丰度比低于银河系典型值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解(U)LIRGs中不同相态气体（电离、热分子）与冰成分的空间分布及物理条件差异，特别是消光特性与冰丰度的关系，以揭示恒星形成与尘埃/冰演化过程。

Method: 使用JWST/NIRSpec积分视场光谱（R~2700，0.97–5.14 μm），结合>25条H复合线与H2转动-振动发射线，以及H2O（~3.02 μm）和CO2（~4.26 μm）冰吸收特征，构建三种新方法分别测定电离气体（A_K^HII）、热分子气体（A_K^H2）的消光，以及H2O和CO2冰柱密度（N_H2O、N_CO2）。

Result: 核区（0.2"半径）消光和冰柱密度显著高于延展区；A_K^HII ≈ 1.5 × A_K^H2；H2O冰柱密度在两相间相近，部分核区达~10^18.81 cm⁻²；CO2/H2O丰度比中位数为~0.10，比银河系低2–3倍。

Conclusion: 电离气体起源于更致密嵌埋区域，而水冰广泛包络电离与热分子气体；最致密核区存在额外冰富集物质；较低的CO2/H2O比可能反映星系环境差异或冰化学演化不同。

Abstract: We investigate the spatially resolved extinction and ice column densities in four nearby interacting (U)LIRGs using JWST/NIRSpec integral field spectroscopy (R$\sim$2700; 0.97--5.14 $μ$m). This wide range encompasses numerous (>25) H recombination and H$_2$ ro-vibrational emission lines, as well as the prominent H$_2$O ($\sim$3.02 $μ$m) and CO$_2$ ($\sim$4.26 $μ$m) ice absorption features. We develop three methods to derive phase-specific extinction for ionized (A$_{\text K}^{\text HII}$) and hot molecular gas (A$_{\text K}^{\text H_2}$), and H$_2$O and CO$_2$ ice column densities (N$_{\text H_2O}$ and N$_{\text CO_2}$), exploiting H and H$_2$ emission lines and ice absorption features. Nuclear regions (0.2" radius) exhibit higher extinction and ice column densities than extended regions, with median A$_{\text K}^{\text HII}$ and A$_{\text K}^{\text H_2}$ of 1.25 and 0.75 in the nuclei, compared to 0.61 and 0.50 in the extended regions. The ionized gas is more extincted than the hot molecular gas, with A$_{\text K}^{\text HII}\approx1.5$ A$_{\text K}^{\text H_2}$, while H$_2$O ice column densities are comparable between phases. In some nuclei, continuum-derived N$_{\text H_2O}$ reaches $\sim10^{18.81}$ cm$^{-2}$, likely due to compact obscured components. These results suggest that ionized gas originates in more deeply embedded regions, whereas water ice envelopes both gas phases, with additional ice-rich material around the most obscured nuclei. The median N$_{\text CO_2}$/N$_{\text H_2O}$ abundance ratio is $\sim0.10$, a factor of 2--3 below typical Galactic values.

</details>


### [3] [Beyond the Disc--Spheroid Dichotomy at $0.6 < z < 8.0$: Evidence for a Morphological Indeterminacy Regime from JWST](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24078)
*Igor Kolesnikov,Reinaldo Ramos de Carvalho,Jeong Hwan Lee,Rodrigo Flores-Freitas,Vitor M. Sampaio,Luiz A. Silva-Lima*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用JWST观测数据和IllustrisTNG模拟，结合非参数MEGG机器学习方法（基于光分布二阶矩、熵、基尼系数和梯度模式分析），系统研究了红移0.6–8.0范围内星系形态分类的一致性与演化；发现z>2.5后参数与非参数分类显著偏离，提出‘形态不确定区’（MIR）概念，表明早期宇宙中大量星系无法被经典盘/椭球分类唯一刻画，反映其动力学年轻、尚未进入哈勃序列的物理本质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 星系形态是星系并合历史的关键示踪器，但在高红移（早期宇宙）下传统形态分类可靠性下降；亟需一种不依赖人工标注或预设光分布模型的客观、稳健分类方法，以揭示真实形态演化规律。

Method: 采用无监督-监督混合机器学习框架，基于非参数MEGG系统（融合光分布二阶矩、Entropy、Gini系数、Gradient Pattern Analysis）对15,567个JWST观测星系及6,099个IllustrisTNG合成星系进行形态量化分类；辅以跨域验证与投影不变性测试评估鲁棒性。

Result: 发现z<2.5时参数与非参数分类基本一致，z>2.5后系统偏离，定义为形态不确定区（MIR）；MIR星系占比达~0.55；其恒星质量中位值（log M⋆/M⊙≈9.7）介于低质量盘（≈9.5）与大质量椭球（≈10.1）之间；MIR分类结果在不同数据集和视线方向上稳定可靠。

Conclusion: 形态不确定是早期宇宙星系组装的固有特征，反映大量动力学尚未弛豫的年轻系统，挑战了将哈勃序列外推至高红移的传统范式；MEGG方法为探索宇宙早期结构形成提供了更普适的形态诊断工具。

Abstract: Galaxy morphology is a fundamental tracer of galaxy assembly, but its characterization becomes increasingly uncertain in the early Universe. We investigate morphological classifications across different methodologies over the redshift range $0.6 < z < 8.0$, using JWST observations of 15,567 galaxies with 6,099 synthetic galaxies from IllustrisTNG. To quantify morphology without relying on visual labels or prescribed light profiles, we use an unsupervised-supervised hybrid machine-learning approach based on the non-parametric MEGG system, which combines the second moment of the light distribution, Entropy, Gini, and Gradient Pattern Analysis. We find that parametric and non-parametric classifications remain broadly consistent at $z < 2.5$, but diverge systematically at higher redshift. We identify this epoch as the Morphological Indeterminacy Regime (MIR), in which classical disc and spheroidal categories no longer provide a unique description of the galaxy population. The fraction of mixed-interpretation galaxies increases with redshift, reaching $F_{\rm MIR} \sim 0.55$ at $z > 2.5$. Cross-domain and projection-invariance tests show that the classifications are stable and insensitive to data set and viewing angle. In the interval $2.5 < z < 8.0$, MIR galaxies occupy an intermediate stellar-mass regime (median; $\log(M_\star/M_\odot) \sim 9.7$), between low-mass discs (median; $\log(M_\star/M_\odot) \sim 9.5$) and massive spheroids (median; $\log(M_\star/M_\odot) \sim 10.1$), while irregular systems have stellar-mass distributions similar to those of MIR galaxies. We interpret these results as evidence that morphological indeterminacy is a characteristic feature of galaxy assembly in the early Universe, reflecting a population of dynamically young systems that have not yet settled into the classical Hubble sequence.

</details>


### [4] [Can Blue UV-Bright Galaxies Drive Reionization at Cosmic Dawn? An $M_{\rm UV}=-19.6$ Galaxy with $f_{\rm esc}\approx0.85$ at $z=9.244$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24092)
*Yunjing Wu,Sijia Cai,Yongda Zhu,Masami Ouchi,Yuichi Harikane,Zheng Cai,Mengtao Tang,Khee-Gan Lee,John D. Silverman,Fengwu Sun,Xiaohui Fan,Zuyi Chen,Minami Nakane,Hiroto Yanagisawa,Angela Adamo,Fuyan Bian,Jaclyn B. Champagne,Dan Coe,Seiji Fujimoto,Enrico Garaldi,Tiger Yu-Yang Hsiao,Xiangyu Jin,Mingyu Li,Yongming Liang,Xiaojing Lin,Guan-Fu Liu,Junjie Mao,Masamune Oguri,Takafumi Tsukui,Feige Wang,Jinyi Yang*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用JWST/NIRSpec对高红移（z=9.244）透镜星系EBG-1进行深度光谱观测，发现其具有极蓝的连续谱和微弱的发射线，推断其低金属丰度和高电离光子逃逸率（f_esc ≈ 0.85），支持晚期再电离模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解宇宙再电离时期主导电离源的性质，特别是高红移星系的金属丰度、化学丰度比及电离光子逃逸能力。

Method: 基于JWST/NIRSpec G235M光谱（14小时积分），测量C III]、Hβ和[O III]λ5007等发射线的等效宽度，并结合BEAGLE代码模拟合成光谱，联合拟合UV连续谱斜率与发射线强度以约束电离光子逃逸率f_esc。

Result: 获得C III] EW < 8.4 Å（3σ）、Hβ EW = 256.2 ± 122.2 Å（边缘探测）、[O III]λ5007 EW = 882.8 ± 180.6 Å（5σ）；推得氧丰度12+log(O/H) = 7.16 ± 0.25（≈3% Z⊙），碳氧比log(C/O) < −0.88（3σ）；模型拟合给出f_esc ≳ 0.75，最佳值≈0.85。

Conclusion: EBG-1作为z > 9的蓝星系代表，虽金属丰度低、气体电离强，但具备极高电离光子逃逸率，表明类似UV明亮星系可能在再电离晚期起关键作用。

Abstract: We revisit EBG-1, a gravitationally lensed galaxy at $z = 9.244$, using newly obtained deep (14 hr) JWST/NIRSpec G235M ($R \sim 1000$) spectroscopy. EBG-1 is one of the bluest galaxies observed at $z > 9$. Along with its remarkably blue continuum of $β= -2.99 \pm 0.15$, we find very weak nebular emission from EBG-1. We obtain a $3σ$ upper limit of EW $< 8.4$ Angstrom for C III], a marginal H$β$ measurement (EW $= 256.2 \pm 122.2$ Angstrom), and a $5σ$ detection of [O III] $λ5007$ (EW $= 882.8 \pm 180.6$ Angstrom). These lines suggest an oxygen abundance of $12 + \log(\mathrm{O/H}) = 7.16 \pm 0.25$ ($\simeq 3\% Z_{\odot}$) and $\log(\mathrm{C/O}) < -0.88$ ($3σ$), placing EBG-1 on the C/O-metallicity sequence traced by lower-redshift galaxies. To constrain the escape fraction ($f_{\rm esc}$), we compare the observed UV slope and emission-line equivalent widths with synthetic spectra generated using BEAGLE. The models favor $f_{\rm esc} \gtrsim 0.75$, with $f_{\rm esc} \approx 0.85$ providing the best overall agreement with the observations. These results suggest that relatively UV-bright galaxies like EBG-1 can achieve the exceptionally high ionizing efficiencies supported by late reionization scenarios.

</details>


### [5] [Spiraling into the `snail' cluster MCXC J0157.4-0550](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24360)
*Chao Geng,Chong Ge,Ming Sun,Li Ji,Guilin Liu,Zhicheng He,Haiguang Xu,John ZuHone,Paul Nulsen,Craig Sarazin,Nobuhiro Okabe*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文通过深度Chandra观测研究了星系团MCXC J0157.4-0550中一个由潮汐作用引发的巨型螺旋冷锋（‘蜗牛’子团），揭示其起源于气体被星系团介质剥离并经轨道旋转缠绕形成，而非传统核心振荡；结合表面亮度与光谱分析，确认密度跳变约3倍、温度不连续，并利用高斯梯度模滤波和方位角分析识别出约90 kpc尺度的开尔文–亥姆霍兹不稳定性结构，从而限定了ICM有效粘滞系数（<0.59倍Spitzer值）和界面磁场强度（<5.9 μG）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解星系团并合过程中大尺度气体动力学与小尺度等离子体输运过程，特别是非经典冷锋（如潮汐缠绕型）的形成机制及其对ICM物理参数（如粘滞、磁场）的约束能力。

Method: 利用130 ks深度Chandra X射线数据，开展表面亮度与空间分辨光谱分析（四象限）、热力学绘图；采用高斯梯度模（GGM）滤波与方位角表面亮度分析识别开尔文–亥姆霍兹不稳定性结构；结合流体力学理论模型反推ICM粘滞系数与磁场上限。

Result: 发现J0157子团存在迄今最大之一的螺旋冷锋（延伸~260 kpc）；确认密度跳变~3倍、显著温度不连续及低熵剥离气体结构；识别出~90 kpc尺度的KH不稳定性纹状结构；由此导出ICM有效粘滞系数≲0.59倍Spitzer值、界面磁场≲5.9 μG。

Conclusion: J0157是研究并合驱动宏观气体动力学与微观等离子体输运的理想实验室；其冷锋成因属潮汐缠绕而非核心振荡，拓展了冷锋分类与物理诠释框架；KH不稳定性观测为ICM粘滞与磁场提供了关键直接限制。

Abstract: We present a deep Chandra (130 ks) study of a striking, tidally induced cold front in a subcluster merging with the galaxy cluster MCXC J0157.4-0550. The subcluster, nicknamed the `snail' (J0157), displays an extensive spiral structure that extends to $\sim 260$ kpc --- one of the largest spiral cold fronts identified to date. Surface brightness and spectral analysis across four sectors reveal a consistent density jump of a factor of $\sim 3$ across the front, together with significant temperature discontinuities, and thermodynamic mapping further delineates the coherent, low-entropy structure of the stripped gas. Unlike classical sloshing cold fronts, the spiral in J0157 arises not from oscillation of a displaced cool core, but from subcluster gas that has been ram pressure stripped and tidally wound into a spiral by orbital rotation. Applying Gaussian Gradient Magnitude filtering and azimuthal surface-brightness analysis, we identify quasi-periodic corrugations along the front consistent with candidate Kelvin-Helmholtz (KH) instabilities, with a characteristic scale of $\sim 90$ kpc. The presence of these KH rolls constrains the effective ICM viscosity to $\lesssim 0.59$ times the Spitzer value and the magnetic field strength at the interface to $\lesssim 5.9\ μ$G. These results establish J0157 as a useful laboratory for probing macro-scale merger-induced gas dynamics and micro-scale plasma transport processes in the intracluster medium.

</details>


### [6] [Statistical Strong Lensing V. Inference with Microlensed Quasars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24423)
*Shuo Zhang,Alessandro Sonnenfeld*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文将微引力透镜效应纳入强引力透镜星系的分层统计模型中，利用类星体像的流量测量，在考虑强透镜选择效应的前提下，有效缓解恒星初始质量函数（IMF）与暗物质分布之间的简并性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微引力透镜可提供透镜星系恒星成分的直接信息，有助于打破恒星初始质量函数（IMF）与暗物质分布之间的简并；但目前尚缺乏能同时前向建模微透镜数据并计入强透镜选择效应的统计框架。

Method: 将微透镜效应整合进Paper III提出的分层模型中，在219个双像类星体模拟样本（由球对称透镜产生）上测试该方法，并对比是否建模微透镜对参数推断的影响。

Result: 建模微透镜后，图像流量推断准确恢复了输入的前景星系总体参数，尤其在μα–μ5平面上约束显著增强：μα的68%可信区间从0.16 dex缩小至0.09 dex，且其与μ5的简并性大幅减弱；而忽略微透镜则导致参数估计严重偏差。

Conclusion: 本工作是微透镜数据高精度建模的第一步；未来应用于真实样本需放弃球对称假设，并采用更真实的微透镜放大模型。

Abstract: Microlensing of strongly lensed quasars provides direct information on the stellar component of lens galaxies, and can help break the degeneracy between the stellar initial mass function (IMF) and the dark matter. However, a statistical framework to forward model microlensing data while accounting for strong lensing selection effects is still missing. We incorporated microlensing effects into the hierarchical model of Paper III, and investigated to what extent flux measurements of strongly lensed quasars can break the degeneracy between the stellar IMF and the dark matter distribution. We tested the method on a mock sample of 219 doubly imaged quasars, lensed by circularly symmetric lenses. When microlensing effects are modelled, the inference using image fluxes recovers the input foreground population parameters and provides tighter constraints than the inference without flux constraints. The improvement is clearest in the $μ_α-μ_5$ plane, where $μ_α$ describes the mean stellar population synthesis mismatch parameter $\logα_{\rm SPS}$ and $μ_5$ describes the mean projected dark matter mass within $5\,{\rm kpc}$. The 68% credible interval on $μ_α$ shrinks from 0.16 dex to 0.09 dex, with the degeneracy between $μ_α$ and $μ_5$ significantly reduced. By contrast, fitting the image fluxes without accounting for microlensing leads to large biases on the inferred parameters. This work is a first step towards an accurate modelling of microlensing data. Application to real lens samples will require dropping the circular symmetry assumption and adopting more realistic models for the microlensing magnification.

</details>


### [7] [Newly Identified Planetary Nebula Candidates in M31 using Deep-Learning Search of Hubble Space Telescope Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24554)
*Yiğit C. Atılgan,Aleyna Demirci,Nazım Aksaker,Ammar Abbas Elmas,Mehmet Sarıgül,Aysun Akyüz*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于深度学习（Faster R-CNN）的方法，在哈勃望远镜宽波段图像中高效搜寻M31星系盘中的行星状星云（PNe），克服了传统色-星等选择纯度低、现有星表不完整等问题；该方法在测试区召回73%已知PNe，显著优于经典方法，并发现多个新候选体，其中一颗被确认为真实PN，验证了方法有效性。


<details>

Abstract: We present the DARWIN project, a suite of cosmological simulations with RAMSES to study dwarf-galaxy formation and evolution across diverse environments. Abstract: While known interstellar scattering screens are largely confined to the Galactic disk, the scattering properties of the circumgalactic medium (CGM) in the deep Galactic halo have remained unexplored. Abstract: The Central Molecular Zone (CMZ) of our Galaxy is a prime laboratory for interstellar chemistry because of its extreme conditions, such as shocks and high cosmic-ray ionisation rates. Abstract: Axion identification in multi-phase models is subtle because local mass eigenstates do not by themselves determine global properties such as the fundamental period and domain-wall number. In this paper, we develop a route to identify the axion in a broad class of multi-phase models, including DFSZ-like theories. The construction involves two reductions: gauge identification and integrating out heavy phase fields. We study these reductions explicitly in two representative two-phase models. Gauge identification reduces the compact field space of the phase fields to a quotient space by identifying points connected by gauge transformations, while integrating out heavy phase fields restricts the low-energy dynamics to nonlinear orbits. Under the stated assumptions and within the validity of the derivative expansion, the physical low-energy field space forms a finite union of $S^1$. The axion is identified as the effective field on an $S^1$ orbit and coincides locally with the lightest mass eigenstate near the vacuum. Its fundamental period and domain-wall number are well-defined and can be determined from the original Lagrangian. Nevertheless, the reduction to a single axion does not capture all properties of topological defects in the original multi-phase theory. In particular, axion strings can retain nontrivial gauge flux, while special defect configurations can involve non-negligible spacetime derivatives of the heavy phase fields and invalidate the single-field reduction. These effects represent fundamental differences between multi-phase and single-phase theories at the level of topological defects.

Abstract: Neutrinos interact with matter exclusively through the weak force, resulting in extremely small cross sections that are difficult to measure. Formally defined as gauge-invariant matrix elements, the determination of FFs is achieved through global QCD analyses that synthesize world data from three primary processes: electron-positron annihilation, semi-inclusive deep inelastic scattering, and proton-proton collisions. This chapter covers the theoretical foundations of FFs, including their operator definitions and properties, the experimental probes used to constrain them, and the methodologies of modern global analyses. The discussion is extended to more advanced topics, including dihadron FFs, the three-dimensional picture of hadronization provided by spin- and transverse-momentum-dependent FFs, and the modification of fragmentation in a nuclear medium, which is key to probe their fundamental properties.

Abstract: We study quark matter at high isospin asymmetry in the limit of vanishing baryon density, a state of matter governed by the condensation of charged pions. In the weak-coupling limit, this state of matter can be reliably described by conventional perturbative methods where a trivial ground state is assumed. However, since the condensate of charged pions generates a gap in the excitation spectrum of the quarks with a non-perturbative dependence on the strong coupling, corrections to the results from conventional perturbative methods arise. This situation is very reminiscent of the one encountered in isospin-symmetric quark matter at high baryon densities which is expected to be a color superconductor. In the present work, we calculate the thermodynamic properties of superfluid quark matter at high isospin asymmetry, zero temperature, and zero baryon density at next-to-leading order (NLO) in the coupling in the presence of a finite gap. We work in the limit of two massless quark flavors and further assume that the gap is small compared to the isospin chemical potential. Similar to the situation in color-superconducting quark matter at high baryon densities, we find that the gap-induced corrections lead to an increase of the pressure and the speed of sound above their values obtained for non-superfluid quark matter. Notably, starting from asymptotically high isospin asymmetries, these corrections lead to a systematic change of the slope of the speed of sound when the isospin asymmetry is decreased. We compare our results with recent lattice QCD and Dyson-Schwinger equation studies and find good agreement in the regimes where these methods are applicable.

Abstract: Transverse-momentum-dependent (TMD) parton distributions provide a three-dimensional description of the momentum structure of hadrons. They are probed in proton-proton and proton-nucleus collisions at the LHC and RHIC, and are a central part of the physics program of the future Electron-Ion Collider (EIC). While TMD factorization is well established at moderate Bjorken $x$, the small-$x$ regime requires a different treatment, since large logarithms of $1/x$ must be resummed and non-linear effects due to high gluon densities become important. The Color Glass Condensate (CGC) effective theory provides a natural framework to describe TMDs in this saturation regime and to connect them with standard TMD factorization. This chapter reviews quark and gluon TMDs at small $x$, covering operator definitions, gauge-link structures, and Wilson-line correlators in the CGC for unpolarized and polarized distributions. We discuss factorization for inclusive and diffractive observables, highlighting the role of process dependence and the emergence of a limited set of Wilson-line operators. We then summarize the evolution of these distributions, namely the resummation of logarithms of $1/x$ by the Balitsky-Kovchegov and JIMWLK equations, and of Sudakov logarithms of the hard scale through Collins-Soper-Sterman evolution. We also review TMDs beyond the eikonal approximation, including the sub-eikonal Wilson-line operators that give access to helicity and other spin-dependent distributions, their double-logarithmic small-$x$ evolution and the resulting asymptotics, and the sub-eikonal corrections to the eikonal distributions, which provide a first step toward the moderate-$x$ regime. Finally, we discuss phenomenological implications for present and future experiments, with emphasis on the EIC, and outline the theoretical challenges toward a unified description of TMDs.

</details>

Abstract: We study the charge balance function $B(Δη,Δφ)$ and the charge-dependent transverse-momentum balance function $P_{2}^{\rm CD}(Δη,Δφ)$ in proton--proton ($pp$) collisions at $\sqrt{s}=13.6$~TeV using \texttt{PYTHIA~8.315} with the Monash tune. Flattenicity is employed as an event-shape observable to probe how these correlations depend on the topology of particle production. Both observables exhibit pronounced near-side correlations around $(Δη,Δφ)\approx(0,0)$. A clear dependence on flattenicity is also observed: jet-like events (low flattenicity) generally exhibit stronger and narrower near-side correlations than isotropic events (high flattenicity). These results demonstrate the complementary nature of the two observables and the potential of flattenicity-based event selection for studying particle-production mechanisms in high-multiplicity $pp$ collisions.

Abstract: This work is motivated by two independent considerations. The first is that KNO scaling is observed in the central rapidity region ($|η|<0.5$, for example) and violated when the fragmentation region is included ($|η|<3.4$, for example). The second is that, in the CGC framework, the $k_T$-factorization formalism is more appropriate to describe particle production in the central region and the hybrid formalism is more appropriate for the fragmentation region. The former contains small-x gluon interactions, where the latter introduce also collinear gluons and valence quarks in the dynamics. These two considerations put together raise the natural question: are collinear gluons and valence quarks responsible for KNO scaling violation? To answer this question we build a two-component CGC model which is able to reproduce the measured pseudorapidity distributions over all the available range as well as multiplicity distributions in pp collisions at LHC energies. Abstract: We investigate dark matter (DM) interpretations of the Galactic Center Excess (GCE) using 17.7 years of Fermi-LAT data and the FL16Y source catalog. For Model X, the best-fitting diffuse-emission model in our template analysis including a DM component, the dSph limits exclude the 68\% CL GCE regions for $b\bar b$, $c\bar c$, light quarks, gluons, and effective $4b$ and $2b2τ$. Parts of the $τ^+τ^-$ and effective $4τ$ regions remain compatible, although their best-fit points exceed these limits. Heavy two-body final states are less constrained but fit the GCE less well. Mediator masses affect both the preferred regions and the constraints. Part of the 68\% CL $4b$ cascade region remains below the dSph limits for $r=m_{\rm mediator}/m_{\rm DM}\lesssim0.4$. Among the four-body benchmarks, $4τ$ retains a comparatively large preferred region compatible with dSph and antiproton bounds. With the adopted BIG propagation model, antiproton bounds are generally weaker but constrain part of the $4b$ cascade region. Finally, we examine a secluded singlet fermionic DM benchmark with a predominantly four-bottom cascade.

Abstract: In the standard cosmological paradigm, the gravitational wave (GW) spectrum from a cosmic string network is well defined. Abstract: We formulate the Froggatt-Nielsen mechanism in the framework of non-invertible symmetries obtained by $\mathbb{Z}_2$ gauging of $\mathbb{Z}_N$ or $U(1)$. While the non-invertible selection rules can constrain the texture-zero structure of fermion mass matrices, they do not by themselves determine the magnitudes of the allowed couplings. Introducing a flavon field allows the fusion rules to fix the leading powers of the small parameter $\varepsilon=\langle S\rangle/Λ$, thereby determining both the texture zeros and the orders of magnitude of the nonzero entries up to $\mathcal{O}(1)$ coefficients. We have constructed explicit quark and lepton models and study their predictions for the fermion mass and mixing hierarchies through analytical estimates and numerical analysis. In particular, a benchmark model with nine texture zeros in the quark sector can well accommodate the quark masses and mixing parameters with non-Hermitian Yukawa matrices. Its lepton sector admits a minimal type-I seesaw realization with normal neutrino mass ordering and one massless neutrino. These results provide a common symmetry framework for fermion mass hierarchies and texture zeros.

Abstract: Weak magnetism, introduced by Gell-Mann as a hallmark consequence of the conserved vector current (CVC) hypothesis, has remained unobserved in the decay of the free neutron, the simplest $β$-decay system. The reason is that the relevant observable, the $β$ asymmetry, cannot separate the weak-magnetism form factor $g_M$ from the axial-to-vector ratio $λ$. We show that this degeneracy is lifted by combining the $β$ asymmetry measurements with the neutron lifetime and the $\mathcal{F}t$ value of superallowed $0^+\to0^+$ decays. Current data yield $g_M = 4.47(87)$, in agreement with the CVC prediction of $4.71$ and excluding the absence of weak magnetism at the $5.1σ$ level. Importantly, the significance remains between $5.0σ$ and $6.1σ$ for all considered variations of the neutron dataset, despite its well-known internal tensions. We further show that, under present experimental conditions, a full analysis of the energy dependence of the asymmetry would improve the $g_M$ extraction by only a few percent. These results constitute the first observation of weak magnetism in the decay of the free neutron nearly seven decades after Gell-Mann's prediction.

Abstract: Galaxy-galaxy lensing (GGL) measurements around galaxy samples in different environments provide valuable information about the galaxy-halo connection. In such studies the halo model is commonly used to estimate physical parameters from the lensing signal, but the impact of environment is generally not considered. Our aim is to adapt the halo model to allow a density-split GGL analysis and to understand the environmental dependence of the elements of the model. We use the FLAMINGO suite of cosmological hydrodynamical simulations to construct samples of halos in underdense and overdense regions. Environment density is assigned to halos based on halo counts within an aperture, employing different aperture sizes to probe both local and large scales. We utilize spherical apertures with radii between $1 h^{-1} {\rm Mpc}$ and $10 h^{-1} {\rm Mpc}$. We find a strong dependence on environment for the halo mass function (HMF) and the halo bias. To account for this, we propose an empirical modification that can be applied to existing HMF prescriptions. We find that after a density split, the halo bias is approximately constant with mass. There is also an environmental dependence for the stellar-to-halo-mass relation (SHMR), the conditional stellar mass function, and the mass-concentration relation, all of which can be addressed with existing models. We evaluate the impact of overlooking environment on halo occupation distribution (HOD) parameter inference, finding that it leads to biased estimations of the SHMR and the satellite fraction. These results emphasize the need to review the assumptions adopted in the halo model when working with samples of galaxies binned by environment, and consequently, by galaxy properties that correlate with environment, such as morphology and color.

Result: 随机模型使电离光子发射向低质量晕偏移，导致电离拓扑更碎片化、最大电离区增长延迟；但当总电离光子产率匹配时，全局中性氢分数和21-cm功率谱与确定性模型几乎一致；而不同流体模拟导出的源模型即使产生相似再电离历史，仍展现出明显差异的电离形态和21-cm统计信号。

Abstract: In the standard cosmological model, galaxies forming within dark matter haloes are the primary drivers of the Epoch of Reionization (EoR). The complex physics of galaxy formation introduces scatter in the relation between halo mass and star-formation activity, with implications for the production and escape of ionising photons. In this work, we implement a stochastic source model in our cosmological radiative transfer simulations. We calibrate our model against the high-redshift ultraviolet luminosity function observed by JWST and observational constraints on Lyman-continuum leakage to constrain the ionising escape fraction. We find that stochastic star formation redistributes ionising emissivity toward lower-mass haloes, producing a more fragmented ionisation topology and delaying the growth of the largest ionised regions relative to the deterministic, observationally calibrated model. However, when the two models match total ionising photon production, their global neutral fractions and 21-cm power spectrum remain nearly indistinguishable, despite small differences in the morphology of individual ionised regions. Additionally, we establish a unified framework that lets us apply source models derived from computationally expensive hydrodynamical simulations (SPICE, FIRE-2 and Thesan) and investigate their impact on large-volume reionisation simulations, extending their prescriptions to scales of >100 Mpc. We find that these prescriptions produce distinct ionisation topologies and 21-cm statistical signatures, even when their global reionisation histories are similar. Our results highlight the importance of combining physically motivated galaxy formation prescriptions from hydrodynamical simulations with large-volume radiative transfer simulations to interpret forthcoming 21-cm observations and constrain the sources of cosmic reionisation.

### [75] [Extending SOMPZ beyond Spectroscopic Coverage for Roman Weak Lensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24066)
*Yufei Zhen,Chun-Hao To,Boyan Yin,Junzhou Zhang,Chihway Chang,Finian Ashmead,Brett H. Andrews,Daniel C. Masters,Jeffrey A. Newman,Tianqing Zhang*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱引力透镜宇宙学需要高精度的星系红移分布，但现有光谱样本无法充分覆盖弱透镜源星系的星等–颜色–红移空间，尤其在Roman HLIS深度下需外推，导致系统性偏差。

Method: 基于Cardinal模拟的LSST Y4与Roman HLIS测光数据，量化光谱不完备性对光度红移（photo-z）校准及宇宙学约束的影响；提出Roman-SOMPZ新框架，其中引入UMAP-flow-z——利用UMAP嵌入深场星系颜色、为缺乏光谱代表性的星系表型推断红移。

Conclusion: 光谱不完备性是Roman时代弱透镜红移校准的核心挑战；Roman-SOMPZ及其UMAP-flow-z组件可有效缓解该问题，但实现全宇宙学精度仍需补充观测或方法改进。

Abstract: Precision weak lensing cosmology requires accurate galaxy redshift distributions. However, the spectroscopic samples used to characterize these distributions do not fully span the magnitude--color--redshift space of weak lensing source galaxies, necessitating extrapolation. This challenge will be critical at the depth of the Roman Space Telescope's High Latitude Imaging Survey (Roman HLIS). Using simulated LSST Year 4 and Roman HLIS photometry from the Cardinal simulations, we quantify how spectroscopic incompleteness biases photometric-redshift (photo-z) calibration and cosmological constraints. We find that the standard Self-Organizing Map p(z) (SOMPZ) method produces substantial tomographic bin overlaps and biased source redshift distributions. To address these limitations, we develop Roman-SOMPZ, which uses dedicated individual-galaxy photo-z estimators to supply redshift references for tomographic binning and redshift estimation. For the latter, we introduce UMAP-flow-z, an estimator that uses a UMAP embedding of deep-field galaxy colors to infer redshifts for phenotypes lacking spectroscopic representation. Compared with the standard pipeline, Roman-SOMPZ produces better-separated tomographic bins and lower absolute mean-redshift biases in eight of nine bins. Propagating the estimated redshift distributions into cosmological inference, we find that existing spectroscopy is adequate for early-stage weak lensing analyses in the Roman deep fields. Conversely, full medium-tier cosmology will require further improvements in redshift characterization.

Abstract: Primordial black holes (PBHs) formed from the collapse of curvature perturbations in the early universe are known to have an associated gravitational wave signal sourced by the same scalar perturbations. In this work, we show that there is a complementarity between these gravitational waves and modifications of the 21 cm global signal during the cosmic dark ages due to PBH evaporation. For PBHs in the range $ M_{\rm PBH} \sim 5 \times 10^{13} \, {\rm g} - 10^{17} \, {\rm g}$, there is a correlated signal in gravitational waves in the LISA, deci-Hz, and ground based regimes from $f_{\rm GW} \sim 10^{-3} \, {\rm Hz} - 20 \, {\rm Hz}$ and in the 21 cm global signal from $z \sim 300 - 30$. These observations are entirely independent of each other and within the reach of near-future experiments, making a combined measurement a novel multimessenger probe of primordial physics.

Abstract: Galaxy clusters are sensitive to a rich variety of physics spanning a wide dynamic range, from microphysical to cosmological scales. There is excellent agreement between our estimates for different subsets of the 13,000 ${\rm deg}^2$ dataset, and also with results from prior catalogs and simulations. We use our joint analysis of millimeter-wave and optical data to estimate the halo masses of individual clusters. We also provide lensing profiles, measured for each cluster using overlapping galaxy shape catalogs, to enable additional mass-calibration work from the community. We demonstrate from a suite of tests that spatial inhomogeneity in the quality of the imaging data does not have an appreciable impact on the properties of this catalog. We also test a cylindrical richness observable, which is defined such that it is significantly simpler to model, and show that it has precision competitive with the traditional optical richness observable. All data products and measurements are made publicly available.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱碰撞等离子体（如星系团内介质和太阳风）中湍流发电机的启动阈值——临界磁雷诺数Rm_crit——在超音速条件下尚未明确，而此前研究主要集中于碰撞性MHD情形，亟需在更真实的弱碰撞、高马赫数参数下定量确定Rm_crit。

Result: 测得Rm_crit = 190^{+21}_{-17}（初始磁化）和198^{+16}_{-18}（初始未磁化），二者在误差范围内一致；该值显著高于亚音速弱碰撞情形，也比超音速MHD情形高约1.5倍。

Conclusion: 在超音速弱碰撞等离子体中，湍流发电机的临界磁雷诺数约为190–198，且不依赖初始磁化程度；压缩性增强提高了Rm_crit，意味着超音速弱碰撞环境下湍流磁放大更难发生，这对理解星系团磁场起源等天体物理问题具有重要启示。

Abstract: Weakly-collisional plasmas are found in several turbulent astrophysical environments, such as the Intra-galaxy-cluster medium (ICM) and the solar wind, where magnetic fields can be amplified by the turbulent dynamo. The onset of dynamo action is determined by the critical magnetic Reynolds number, ${\rm Rm}_{\rm crit}$, above which turbulent motions amplify magnetic energy. Although ${\rm Rm}_{\rm crit}$ has been studied extensively in collisional magnetohydrodynamic (MHD) turbulence, its value in supersonic weakly-collisional plasmas remains unknown. Here we determine ${\rm Rm}_{\rm crit}$ using three-dimensional hybrid particle-in-cell (HPIC) simulations of solenoidally driven turbulence with a sonic Mach number $\approx 2$. We vary the magnetic Reynolds number over ${\rm Rm}\approx31$-$1000$ and consider two initial plasma magnetisations, characterised by initial Larmor ratios $r_{\rm Larmor, 0}/L_{\rm box}=1$ and $100$. We find that the magnetic energy grows exponentially at sufficiently large ${\rm Rm}$ and decays when magnetic diffusion dominates at low ${\rm Rm}$. By fitting the magnetic-energy growth rate as a function of ${\rm Rm}$, we measure ${\rm Rm}_{\rm crit}=190^{+21}_{-17}$ and $198^{+16}_{-18}$ for the initially magnetised and unmagnetised plasma, respectively. These values are the same within their uncertainties, indicating that the initial plasma magnetisation does not significantly affect ${\rm Rm}_{\rm crit}$ in the supersonic regime. The measured values are higher than those previously obtained for the subsonic weakly-collisional dynamo, showing that increasing compressibility makes weakly-collisional turbulent dynamo action more difficult to excite. The measured values are also approximately 1.5 times higher than the ${\rm Rm}_{\rm crit}$ value reported for supersonic MHD turbulence.

The integrated Compton-y parameter $Y^{\rm int}$ scales systematically with $Λ$, providing a tSZ proxy accessible to current surveys. On mock populations with triaxial haloes and miscentering, at DES Y3-like and LSST-like density, all three analyses recover the injected gap slope $γ_{\mathrm{cm}}$ with uncertainties that match the scatter between realisations, but not equally precisely: the joint fit scatters 1.2--1.3 times less than the forward-modelled stack, and 2 (DES-like) to 6.5 (LSST-like) times less than the two-step analysis, which is also biased in several other scaling-relation parameters. The mass--concentration slope is consistent with zero in both, as expected from the upturn of the \citet{diemer2019} relation over this mass range. We demonstrate that while the parameter constraints on $w_0$ and $w_a$ remain robust to prior choice (exhibiting only minor shifts of the marginalised posterior towards the $\textit{Maximum A Posteriori}$ in less constraining dataset combinations), the Bayesian evidence is highly sensitive to the use of the Jeffreys prior; replacing uniform priors with the Jeffreys prior reverses the model-selection preference in dataset combinations that favour dynamical dark energy under the uniform prior, while strengthening existing preferences for $Λ$CDM. Abstract: Though galaxy clusters are virialized and mostly in thermodynamic equilibrium, their internal gas thermodynamics are complex. The hot gas is multiphase and spatially inhomogeneous. In this work (part of the ERGO-ML series), we present a direct inference method of the multiphase temperature distribution of the gas in the intra-cluster medium (ICM), applicable over a large range of spatial and mass scales. It works using different spectral resolutions (XRISM-like and Chandra-like) and realistic photon noise. Our method is an application of simulation-based inference. We train a neural-network on X-ray spectra in the range 0.1-10 keV, generated for $\approx 10^5$ sightlines of massive clusters ($M_{\rm 200c} = 10^{14.2-15.2}~\mathrm{M_\odot}$) from the TNG-Cluster cosmological MHD galaxy simulation. On top of that it gives accurate measures of the fraction of sub- and super-virial gas. Our method is capable of extracting the cooler gas phase even if the flux contribution is 3 orders of magnitude below that of the virial gas phase. We then show that we can accurately recover the spatial distribution of multi-phase gas both in relaxed and merging systems. Abstract: Cosmological analyses of Stage-IV galaxy surveys require large ensembles of lightcone mock catalogues, but a full survey-scale $N$-body run costs of order $10^7$ CPU hours, putting such ensembles out of reach. We present a framework that produces survey-scale dark matter lightcones for a typical cost of order $10^3$ CPU hours each, based on the spatial COmoving Lagrangian Acceleration (sCOLA) method. sCOLA decomposes an arbitrarily large volume into small tiles that are evolved independently, with buffer regions and non-periodic boundary conditions on the gravitational potential. We combine it with on-the-fly lightcone building, so that the evolved spacetime region reduces to the observed one: each tile is stopped as soon as the observer's past lightcone has left it, and only the tiles intersecting the region of interest are simulated at all. We further introduce two accuracy improvements to the sCOLA formalism: an LPT buffer that approximates the inflow of particles from outside the sCOLA box, and a second-order perturbative approximation to the gravitational potential at its boundaries; together they remove the large-scale inaccuracies of previous work and allow smaller tiles and buffers. Against a \texttt{Gadget4} reference in a $3.84\,h^{-1}\,\mathrm{Gpc}$ box with $3\,840^3$ particles, up to $k \simeq 0.4\,h\,\mathrm{Mpc}^{-1}$ the sCOLA lightcone reproduces the matter power spectrum to 1%, the velocity divergence power spectrum to 1.5% and the equilateral bispectrum to 2%, with no visible discontinuity at tile boundaries. The run cost $1\,300$ CPU hours, a factor of $2\,600$ below our $\texttt{Gadget4}$ run. Perfect parallelism at this cost brings ensembles of thousands of survey-scale lightcones within reach of a single allocation: $\sim 25\,000$ realisations of the $\textit{Euclid}$ Galactic north cap with a mass resolution of $10^{11}\,\mathrm{M}_\odot$ for $10^{7}$ CPU hours.

</details>

Abstract: The validity of linear perturbation theory and the role of backreaction in single-field inflationary models that enhance power at small scales have recently been under debate. We find that ultraviolet (UV) modes in the Bunch--Davies vacuum can substantially affect backreaction estimates unless removed by adiabatic subtraction. Our mean-field treatment can reproduce recent lattice results, but reveals a spurious sensitivity to UV contributions in some cases. This sensitivity is reduced in the OF model when copious particle production separates the amplified physical modes from the UV vacuum tail, making lattice results more reliable in this regime. In USR, instead, adiabatically subtracted spectra yield milder backreaction on the background evolution than previously inferred.

Motivation: 以往天琴标准烛光研究均基于ΛCDM模型生成模拟数据，本文旨在定量评估背景宇宙学模型选择对模拟目录及后续宇宙学推断的影响。

Result: TQSS1与TQSS2在距离-红移关系上最大相对差异仅1.48%；二者单独约束能力与SN Ia相当；联合多信使数据后，均显著支持动力学暗能量（分别为2.3σ和3.8σ），且黑洞种子模型变化不影响该结论。

Conclusion: 标准烛光宇宙学预测中应考虑不同背景宇宙学模型，否则可能影响对暗能量性质的判断；天琴标准烛光有望成为检验暗能量动力学特性的新独立探针。

Abstract: Space-borne gravitational wave (GW) detectors such as TianQin are expected to detect GW standard sirens for cosmological parameter estimation. All previous studies of TianQin adopted the $Λ$CDM model to generate mock standard siren data. In this work, we quantitatively investigate how the choice of background cosmological model affects mock TianQin standard siren (TQSS) catalogs and the corresponding cosmological implications. Adopting the $Λ$CDM model and a redshift-binned dark energy (DE) reconstruction model (the wbin model), we consider the bright standard sirens from massive black hole binary mergers and generate two mock TQSS catalogs, i.e. TQSS1 and TQSS2, respectively. We investigate the difference of the catalogs and further explore their cosmological implications within the Chevallier-Polarski-Linder (CPL) model, both individually and in combination with DESI DR2 baryon acoustic oscillation (BAO) data, $\textit{Planck}$ 2018 cosmic microwave background (CMB) data, and DESY5 type Ia supernova (SN Ia) data. We find that there are only small differences between TQSS1 and TQSS2 catalogs, with a maximum relative difference of $1.48\%$ in the distance-redshift relation. In addition, either the TQSS1 or TQSS2 catalog alone gives cosmological constraints similar to those obtained from SN data, implying that these two catalogs can serve as a complement to mainstream cosmological observations. Most importantly, we find that both TQSS1+DESI DR2+CMB+DESY5 and TQSS2+DESI DR2+CMB+DESY5 are still expected to favor dynamical DE, with these two combinations showing a preference for dynamical DE at $2.3σ$ and $3.8σ$, respectively. Adopting different massive black hole seed models does not impact the cosmological implications of the mock TQSS catalogs. Our study indicates the importance of considering different cosmological models in the standard siren forecasts for DE.

</details>

### [95] [Missing Light, Misleading Confidence: Optical-Module Readout Loss and Signal Reliability in Neutrino Telescopes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24003)
*Thammarat Yawisit,Pittaya Pannil*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文研究了光学中微子望远镜中基于可用性的质量门控（availability-based quality gates）是否能有效保证信号摘要的通道间统计一致性（coherence），发现在读出丢失（readout loss）情况下，此类门控虽必要但不充分，无法检测脉冲丢失和定时抖动等问题，需引入基于信号的检查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光学中微子望远镜将光模块信号压缩为多通道摘要，读出丢失会导致摘要不可靠；本文探究仅依赖读出可用性标志的质量门控能否可靠认证摘要的统计一致性（coherence），及其失效边界。

Method: 使用公开的IceCube脉冲数据（9484条串记录）与KM3NeT事件KM3-230213A的释放击中数据，在可控读出丢失及真实KM3NeT读出标志重放下，采用2×2实验设计对比递归支持态与直接掩码支持，并在开发集上校准所有阈值。

Result: 支持态未提供额外信息；IceCube中支持门控仅多保留0.16个百分点可用摘要（效应依赖窗口且容忍度变化时符号反转）；KM3NeT中该门控拒绝全部记录；门控仅接受掩码近乎完整记录（含因信号相关丢失导致电荷耗尽而掩码未记录的情况）；脉冲丢失与时间抖动完全逃逸门控，导致高达66%被接受摘要不可靠。

Conclusion: 读出可用性标志是必要但不充分的质量保障，必须辅以基于信号的一致性检查（如coherence检验）才能确保摘要可靠性。

Abstract: Optical neutrino telescopes reduce the photomultiplier signals of an optical module or string segment to compact multichannel summaries, and readout loss can make these summaries unreliable. We asked whether availability-based quality gates, which accept a summary when enough of the readout was live, can certify a summary of inter-channel statistical consistency (called coherence here; not optical coherence) under readout loss, and where they fail. We used public IceCube pulses (9,484 string records) and the released hits of KM3NeT event KM3-230213A with controlled readout loss, together with a replay of real KM3NeT readout flags. In a two-by-two design we compared a published recursive support state with direct mask support, and calibrated every threshold on development records. The support state is a function of the mask and added no information. On IceCube the support-gated rule retained 0.16 percentage points more usable summaries, an effect of the window at which support was read that reversed sign at a looser tolerance; on KM3NeT it accepted nothing. The gates accepted only records with nearly intact masks, including records whose charge had been depleted by signal-dependent loss that the mask does not record. Pulse loss during live acquisition and timing jitter passed every availability gate, which then accepted summaries of which up to 66% were unreliable. Availability flags are therefore necessary but not sufficient, and signal-based checks are needed. This work uses public data and is not an IceCube or KM3NeT Collaboration result.

</details>

Conclusion: 所提RNN系统对伴星光谱添加高度敏感，可用于探测双星混合效应，但尚不能唯一反演伴星贡献比例，需进一步建模或引入更多约束条件。

Abstract: This work presents an initial analysis of the estimation of effective temperature and surface gravity for simulated O-type binary systems using a recurrent neural network. The proposed system uses 34 equivalent-width measurements from the optical region of a stellar spectrum. Two methods were applied to observed IACOB spectra before normalization: the addition of a blackbody continuum and the addition of a second observed spectrum. The first method produces only small differences in the normalized line profiles and in the estimated physical parameters over 3700-7000~Å. In the second method, the secondary spectrum is added with contributions of 5, 10, 20, 30, 50, and 75 per cent, as defined relative to the original primary spectrum. The general comparison includes 192 simulated systems and 1152 modified spectra. For most of these spectra, the estimated effective temperature and surface gravity decrease as the contribution of the secondary component increases. Individual examples using HD14947, HD190429, HD48099, and HD206267 show that this behavior is not the same for every combination: some estimated effective temperatures fall below the estimates for both isolated spectra, and some surface gravity estimates increase. Comparisons with previously published values also depend on the physical parameter and reference considered. The results show that the proposed system is sensitive to the addition of a secondary spectrum, but do not establish a unique contribution for the secondary component of an observed binary.

</details>

Abstract: Methane (CH4) is one of the most enigmatic and potentially consequential trace gases detected on Mars, having demonstrated apparent seasonality, localized plumes, or, depending on who you ask, never conclusively detected at all. While the Martian methane mystery remains unsolved, it has sparked a vibrant area of astrobiology research exploring whether microbial methanogenesis -- one of the simplest and most ancient metabolic pathways -- could plausibly proceed under various extreme physicochemical conditions characteristic of the Martian shallow subsurface. The emerging consensus is a cautious but compelling "possibly". These findings elevate methanogenesis as both a top priority candidate biosignature for extant life detection and a justification for urgently advancing in situ instrumentation capable of definitively detecting CH4 and constraining its origin, biotic or otherwise

Abstract: The rapidsim is a Python package for the RAPID (Representative Approach for Particle-Integrated Disks) numerical simulation code, which is a modular one-dimensional hydrodynamic simulation code for modelling the evolution of protoplanetary discs and embedded dust particles written in C language. The package was developed to provide a simple and platform-independent tool for launching RAPID simulation without having to manually compile the native code and its external dependencies. rapidsim is distributed through the Python Package Index (PyPI) using pre-built binary wheels for Linux and macOS on both Intel and Apple Silicon architectures and for multiple Python versions. To ensure that the most recent package version is always available, an automated continuous-integration and release system synchronizes package versioning with RAPID releases, automatically

*Adrian K. Sinclair,Chris Albert,C. Matt Bradford,Patrick Brown,Sean Bryan,Jesse Coldsmith,Jennifer Corekin,Sumit Dahal,Thomas K. Devlin,Thomas Essinger-Hileman,Logan Foote,Marc Foote,Draisy Friedman,Alessandro Geist,Jason Glenn,Christopher Green,Kyle Helson,Kevin Horgan,Tracee Jamison-Hooks,Elijah Kane,Jared Lucey,Philip Mauskopf,Lynn Miles,Sanetra Bailey Newman,Gerard Quilligan,Cody Roberson,Salman Sheikh,Manuel Vega,Eric Weeks,Christopher Wilson,Travis Wise*

Abstract: We present further development and testing of prototype kinetic inductance detector (KID) readout electronics for the PRobe far-Infrared Mission for Astrophysics (PRIMA). PRIMA is a NASA Probe-class astrophysics mission concept with two science instruments, FIRESS and PRIMAger, which provide spectroscopic and polarimetric imaging from 24 to 261 um. Together these instruments will explore the far-infrared sky with spatial-spectral mapping speeds that exceed previous studies by orders of magnitude, opening a vast discovery space. This increase in mapping speed is achieved by densely packed ultra-sensitive KIDs and cryogenically cooled optics. The PRIMA flight readout electronics will use the build-to-print SpaceCube Mini v3.0 which houses a radiation-tolerant Kintex KU060 field programmable gate array (FPGA). A custom high-speed digitizer board is being designed along with an RF electronics (RFE) board. The RFE was designed to provide programmable gain, band filtering, and RF switching between instruments. The prototype readout electronics, including the RFE, use functionally equivalent parts to the flight parts. In this proceeding we measure the readout loopback noise performance in the second Nyquist zone and timestream cross-talk performance. We present preliminary sweeps with the prototype readout coupled to prototype FIRESS detectors. Lastly, we demonstrate a cosmic ray pulse detection and real-time removal with an algorithm implemented in firmware.

</details>


### [100] [Prospects for submillimeter VLBI of black hole photon rings with a stratospheric balloon-borne telescope](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24396)
*Mayukh Bagchi,Dominic W. Pesce,Felix M. Thiel,Laura M. Fissel,Lindy Blackburn*

TL;DR: 本文探讨了利用平流层气球搭载的亚毫米波VLBI望远镜（工作频段345–690 GHz）补充地面VLBI阵列的科学潜力，重点评估其对Sgr A*和M87*成像、尤其是偏振光子环结构探测的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地面亚毫米VLBI受限于大气吸收和散射，仅能在少数高海拔干燥台址开展；而平流层气球可避开大部分大气干扰，拓展高频观测能力，提升对银心黑洞Sgr A*附近光学薄发射和偏振结构的分辨能力。

Result: 在460 GHz，气球-ALMA基线可在约一半的4月模拟日实现Sgr A*的多次（≥10次）短时（20 s）探测，且多数探测位于n=1偏振光子环主导区；热噪声可在300 s内满足线偏振探测阈值；690 GHz需更苛刻的三倍频相位传递；Fisher分析表明460 GHz环几何约束需气球参与，但精度弱于345 GHz。

Conclusion: 平流层气球VLBI是实现高分辨率460 GHz VLBI观测（尤其针对Sgr A*偏振光子环）的关键补充手段，但需配套32 GHz带宽后端、亚毫米接收机及全台站同步230 GHz相位参考记录。

Abstract: Submillimeter very long baseline interferometry (VLBI) achieves higher angular resolution and probes more optically thin emission near supermassive black holes, with less interstellar scattering toward Sgr A$^*$. Above 345 GHz, however, the atmosphere confines ground-based VLBI to a few high, dry sites. Using 2012-2022 weather, we forecast what a 2.5 m telescope floating at 35-40 km, a successor to the 22 GHz Balloon-borne VLBI Experiment (BVEX) pathfinder, would add to ground-VLBI arrays at 345-690 GHz. The balloon adds little baseline length but reaches higher frequencies, where a fixed baseline is longer in wavelengths. On an ALMA-balloon baseline, Sgr A$^*$ at 460 GHz is at the detection threshold, with ten or more 20 s detections on about half of the simulated April days. These detections lie beyond the polarimetric photon ring transition, where the $n=1$ ring dominates the polarized visibility amplitudes. M87$^*$ at 460 GHz would need frequency phase transfer from a 230 GHz reference to be detected. With phase transfer, most Sgr A$^*$ detections beyond the transition at 460 GHz are on balloon baselines, whose thermal noise meets the linear-polarization threshold within one 300 s scan. At 690 GHz such detections require phase transfer that stays coherent at three times the reference frequency. In Fisher forecasts, a 460 GHz ring-geometry constraint requires balloons but is weaker than at 345 GHz, where balloons add little improvement. High-resolution 460 GHz VLBI science would require a 32 GHz bandwidth backend, submillimeter receivers, and simultaneous 230 GHz reference recording at every station.

</details>


### [101] [Attitude Estimation of Falcon 9 Rocket Body Based on Photometric Characteristics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24596)

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于光度曲线的通用姿态估计方法，利用自动微分和梯度下降实现快速准确的姿态参数估计，克服了现有方法目标特异性的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前空间目标姿态估计方法多依赖目标特定模型（如封闭面元集）和非线性滤波或机器学习，缺乏通用性；而日益增多的空间碎片对在轨卫星和未来任务构成严重威胁，亟需通用、高效的空间目标监视与表征手段。

Method: 提出基于光度曲线的通用姿态估计方法：构建以姿态参数为变量的理论光变模型，定义其与实测光变曲线之间的差异损失函数，并利用自动微分计算梯度，通过梯度下降优化实现姿态估计。

Result: 姿态参数估计时间缩短至15秒，均方误差降至小数点后三位；在第三方数据库上的验证表明该方法具有强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一个通用、高效的空间目标姿态估计框架，显著提升了估计速度与精度，并突破了传统方法的目标特异性限制。

Abstract: The proliferation of space debris, driven by increasingly frequent space activities, poses a severe risk to operational satellites and future missions. Consequently, the surveillance and characterization of space objects have become critical. Current methods for attitude estimation of space objects often rely on target-specific models, such as those with a closed set of facets, and typically employ nonlinear filtering or machine learning techniques. However, these approaches often lack general applicability. This study proposes a more general approach to attitude estimation based on photometric light curves. We define a loss function that quantifies the discrepancy between the measured light curve and a theoretical model parameterized by the attitude parameters. By leveraging automatic differentiation to compute the gradients of this function, we use a gradient descent algorithm to achieve rapid and accurate attitude estimation. The proposed method provides a versatile and efficient framework for attitude estimation, overcoming the target-specificity limitations of existing techniques, with the parameters estimation time reduced to 15 seconds and the mean square error lowered to three decimal places. Validation on the third-party database demonstrates that the method exhibits strong generalization capability.

</details>


### [102] [Inverse-designed tapers to improve throughput of on-chip astrophotonic spectrographs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24699)

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文通过逆向设计方法优化天体光子光谱仪中阵列波导光栅（AWG）与单模光纤之间的耦合结构，以提升整体光通量，从而增强信噪比并降低成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前片上天体光子光谱仪在不同组件（尤其是输入/输出光纤接口）间存在显著耦合损耗，限制了整体光通量和性能。

Method: 采用两类受工艺约束的逆向设计方法：一是边界形状优化的锥形耦合器；二是设计区域内材料分布优化的拓扑优化；均基于Tidy3D仿真平台，使用锗掺杂二氧化硅芯层与纯二氧化硅包层，并匹配SMF-28单模光纤。

Result: 成功设计出高效率的AWG-光纤耦合结构，显著降低界面损耗，提升光谱仪整体光通量，为下一代地基与空间望远镜提供高性能、低成本光子仪器新路径。

Conclusion: 逆向设计是提升天体光子光谱仪性能的有效手段，可突破传统几何直觉限制，实现更高吞吐量与信噪比，推动未来大型望远镜光谱设备小型化与稳定化发展。

Abstract: Astrophotonics offers a pathway towards compact and stable spectrographs for future large telescopes. The size of these spectrographs does not scale with the diameter of the telescope aperture; thus, they offer a cost-effective way to achieve astronomy science requirements compared to traditional optical spectrographs. A major limitation of current on-chip astrophotonic spectrographs is the significant loss incurred at interfaces between distinct components, particularly at the input and output fiber interfaces, limiting overall throughput. In this work, we investigate inverse-designed architectures to optimize transitions between these components to improve throughput. Inverse design formulates device design as an optimization problem, enabling non-intuitive geometries that can achieve high performance. We focus on optimizing coupling between an arrayed waveguide grating (AWG) spectrograph and an input single-mode fiber. Our approach explores two classes of fabrication-constrained inverse design. The first is shape optimization, which optimizes the geometry of the boundary of a coupling taper. The second is topology optimization, which optimizes the material distribution in a design region to gradually transform the optical mode between components. Both methods are implemented using the Tidy3D package from Flexcompute. We use germanium-doped silica for the core of the photonic components and silica as the cladding, and we use an SMF-28 fiber as input. This work demonstrates a pathway to improve astrophotonic spectrographs through computational inverse-design methods. Leveraging higher throughput, we can improve signal-to-noise ratios offered by photonic devices and position ourselves to design high-performance, cost-effective instrumentation for the next generation of telescopes, both on the ground and in space.

</details>


### [103] [An Enhanced Intersecting-Lines Method for Optical Loss Characterization in SIS Heterodyne Receivers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24838)
*François Joint,Magnus Strandberg,Denis Meledin,Igor Lapkin,Sven-Erik Ferm,Mathias Fredrixon,Leif Helldner,Alexei Pavolotsky,Erik Sundin,Victor Belitsky,Vincent Desmaris*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Y因子测量的数值化直线法（相交线法），用于从标准热/冷负载测量中分离提取SIS混频器前端的射频输入噪声和光学损耗，实现了对全频带内频率分辨的输入损耗表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统热/冷负载测量难以单独提取SIS混频器前端的射频损耗，因其接收机噪声包含输入光学系统、混频器及中频链路的综合贡献。

Method: 在本地振荡器（LO）泵浦功率从欠泵浦到过泵浦范围内扫描，获取各中频（IF）频点对应的热/冷中频谱；对每个频点构建输出功率与输入负载温度的线性关系族，计算所有线对交点并分析‘交点云’以估计等效射频输入噪声；在全IF带宽和多个LO调谐点重复该过程。

Result: 实现了频率分辨的输入光学损耗表征，将直线法转化为可复现的接收机表征框架，并支持低损耗前端配置下的相对光学损耗比较。

Conclusion: 该数值化直线法有效克服了传统Y因子法无法解耦多源噪声的局限，为毫米波/太赫兹异质结接收机前端优化提供了可靠、定量的损耗分析工具。

Abstract: Accurate knowledge of radio-frequency (RF) losses in front of superconductor--insulator--superconductor (SIS) mixers is essential for optimizing millimeter- and terahertz heterodyne receivers. However, such losses are difficult to extract from conventional hot/cold-load measurements because the measured receiver noise combines contributions from the input optics, the mixer, and the intermediate-frequency chain. In this work, we present a numerical implementation of the straight-line, or intersecting-lines, method for estimating input-referred RF noise and optical loss from standard Y-factor measurements. Hot/cold IF spectra are acquired while sweeping the local oscillator (LO) pumping level from under-pumped to over-pumped operation, so that each IF frequency bin gives a family of linear relations between receiver output power and input load temperature. We evaluate all pairwise intersections between these lines and analyze the crossing cloud to obtain the effective RF input noise. We repeat the analysis across the IF band and multiple LO tunings which provides a frequency-resolved characterisation of the input losses over the full band. Our implementation turns the straight-line technique into a reproducible framework for receiver characterisation and relative optical-loss comparison in low-loss front-end configurations.

</details>


### [104] [A 4-20 GHz Superconducting 90deg Hybrid Using Capacitively Loaded Coupled Lines for 2SB Heterodyne Receivers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24859)
*François Joint,Max Behrens,Denis Meledin,Cristian Lopez,Magnus Strandberg,Sven-Erik Ferm,Mathias Fredrixon,Leif Helldner,Alexei Pavolotsky,Erik Sundin,Igor Lapkin,Victor Belitsky,Vincent Desmaris*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文设计并表征了一种工作在4-20 GHz的宽带超导90度中频混合耦合器，用于边带分离（2SB）外差接收机，采用电容加载的边缘耦合传输线结构，避免了Lange耦合器所需的空气桥互连，在保持低损耗和良好正交平衡的同时实现紧凑、超导的集成方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为提升毫米波与亚毫米波天文观测中宽带谱线巡天和高红移谱线搜寻的频谱抓取能力，需发展具有宽瞬时带宽的边带分离（2SB）接收机，而高性能中频（IF）混合耦合器是其关键组件。

Method: 采用薄膜铌（niobium）工艺实现电容加载的边缘耦合传输线结构的超导90°中频混合耦合器，替代传统需空气桥的Lange耦合器；在低温环境下使用矢量网络分析仪（VNA）进行幅相不平衡度表征。

Result: 实测表明，在4–20 GHz全频带内，幅度与相位不平衡度分别优于0.6 dB和6°；应用于2SB接收机时，图像抑制比达10–20 dB，验证了其对实际系统性能的支持。

Conclusion: 该混合耦合器为宽瞬时带宽2SB接收机提供了高性能、紧凑、低损耗的超导中频解决方案，有望推动下一代毫米/亚毫米波天文观测设备的发展。

Abstract: We report the design and cryogenic characterisation of a broadband superconducting 90deg intermediate-frequency (IF) hybrid covering 4 to 20 GHz for sideband-separating (2SB) heterodyne receivers. The hybrid is implemented in thin-film niobium using capacitively loaded edge coupled transmission lines, avoiding the air-bridge interconnections required in Lange-coupler layouts while retaining a compact superconducting implementation with low loss and good quadrature balance. Cryogenic measurements using Vector Network Analyser (VNA) show amplitude and phase imbalance better than 0.6 dB and 6deg, respectively, across the full band. When used in a 2SB receiver, this level of IF balance is consistent with the observed image-rejection performance in the 10-20 dB range, for the full receiver. The design supports the development of wide-instantaneous-bandwidth 2SB receivers for millimeter and submillimeter observatories, improving spectral grasp for broadband line surveys and high-redshift line searches.

</details>


### [105] [MACE: Multigroup-Multiangle Integrated Thermal Compton Scattering Kernel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25117)
*Itamar Giron,Menahem Krief,Elad Steinberg*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套高精度的康普顿散射多群多角度辐射输运计算用表格，通过一种自适应嵌套数值积分方法生成，并经过多种方式验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为满足多群、多角度辐射输运计算中对高精度、可复现且易用的康普顿散射数据的需求，现有表格在参数空间某些区域（如反冲峰、热展宽区、前向弹性散射边界层）精度不足。

Method: 开发了一种确定性数值积分方法：对点态康普顿核进行鲁棒数值求值，并采用自适应嵌套高斯求积，其积分区间根据康普顿反冲峰、热展宽及前向近弹性散射边界层动态调整。

Result: 生成了覆盖入射/散射能量群与角度区间的精确热康普顿核积分表；经与已有核剖面、角/能矩、平均自由程、多群再分布数据及独立蒙特卡洛估计器比对，验证了其高精度与可靠性。

Conclusion: 该表格为辐射输运领域提供了准确、可复现、即用型的康普顿再分布矩阵和角矩数据，显著提升了相关模拟的物理保真度与计算稳健性。

Abstract: We present a comprehensive set of accurate Compton-scattering tables for use in multigroup, multiangle radiation-transport calculations. The tables contain the exact thermal Compton kernel integrated over incident-energy groups, scattered-energy groups, and angular bins. To generate them, we develop a deterministic integration method in which the pointwise kernel is evaluated with a robust numerical treatment that remains accurate across challenging regions of parameter space. The multidimensional integrals are computed using nested quadratures whose panels adapt to the Compton recoil ridge, its thermal broadening, and the nearly elastic forward-scattering boundary layer. We validate the tabulated results against published kernel profiles, angular and energy moments, mean free paths, multigroup redistribution data, and an independent Monte Carlo estimator. The resulting tables provide the radiation-transport community with accurate, reproducible, and readily usable Compton redistribution matrices and angular moments.

</details>


### [106] [Characterization of integrating-sphere systematic effects for photon calibration of the KAGRA gravitational wave detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25581)
*Shingo Hido,Misato Onishi,Shinji Miyoki,Takahiro Sawada,Takahiro Yamamoto,Takayuki Tomaru,Yoshiki Moriwaki,Dan Chen*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本研究通过实验表征KAGRA引力波探测器光子校准系统中积分球传感器对入射光束角度、偏振和位置的响应，识别并量化了此前未建模的系统性误差源，显著提升了校准不确定度模型与实测散射的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在KAGRA第四次观测运行（O4a）初期，光子校准测量出现超出预期的散射，其物理根源未被识别，仅经验性地反映在报告的不确定度中。

Method: 实验测量积分球对入射光束的入射角、偏振态和光束位置三个参数的响应，并结合每次校准配置中积分球放置的可重复性，评估并纳入相应系统性不确定度到O4a Pcal-X校准的不确定度模型中。

Result: 加入这些新识别的系统效应后，尤其在KAGRA端站校准参数中，卡方值从高达10^3降至约1至几，显著改善了估计不确定度与观测散射之间的一致性。

Conclusion: 入射光束条件的变化是此前未解释散射的主要来源；该工作建立了更符合物理实际的光子校准功率不确定度评估框架，并为未来通过更好控制光束条件来降低校准不确定度提供了基础。

Abstract: Photon calibration (Pcal) systems are essential for accurately reconstructing gravitational-wave strain in laser interferometric detectors. In the KAGRA Pcal system, the laser power applied to the end test mass is measured using integrating-sphere power sensors. During the first part of the fourth observing run (O4a), repeated calibration measurements showed scatter larger than expected from the uncertainties assigned to individual measurements. Although this excess scatter was empirically reflected in the previously reported calibration uncertainties, its physical origin was not identified. In this study, we experimentally characterize the response of the integrating spheres used in the KAGRA calibration chain with respect to three incident-beam conditions: incidence angle, polarization, and beam position. Based on these measurements and the reproducibility of the integrating-sphere placement in each calibration configuration, we evaluate the corresponding systematic uncertainties and explicitly incorporate them into the uncertainty model for the O4a Pcal-X calibration measurements. The inclusion of these previously unmodeled effects substantially improves the consistency between the estimated uncertainties and the observed scatter, particularly for calibration parameters measured at the KAGRA end station, where the reduced chi-square values decrease from values as large as $10^3$ to values of order unity to several. These results indicate that variations in the incident-beam conditions of integrating-sphere power sensors account for a substantial part of the previously unexplained scatter and establish a more physically motivated framework for evaluating Pcal laser-power calibration uncertainty. They also provide a basis for future reduction of the calibration uncertainty through improved control of the incident-beam conditions.

</details>


### [107] [Plate-YOLO: An End-to-End Object Detection Framework for Robust Astrometric Registration of Historical Plates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25677)
*Quanfeng Xu,Zhengjun Shang,Shiyin Shen,Yong Yu,Mingyang Ma,Zhenghong Tang,Meiting Yang,Jing Yang,Jianhai Zhao*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出Plate-YOLO框架，结合源检测与恒星分类，专为退化历史天文底片设计，显著提升自动天体测量定标成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史天文底片具有长时序优势，但因退化和结构伪影导致传统自动化天体测量校准困难。

Method: 基于YOLOv5改进，引入Space-to-Depth特征重排、SimAM注意力机制、高分辨率P2检测头及归一化Wasserstein距离损失；采用Gaia引导的渐进式微调策略，利用已解算底片构建新档案标签。

Result: 在150张传统流程失败的UBAI底片上，中文预训练模型成功解算40张，目标档案微调后又恢复106张，总恢复率达97.3%；单张底片源提取耗时6.61秒（优于SExtractor的16.73秒）。

Conclusion: Plate-YOLO有效适配退化历史底片，大幅提升了源检测与后续天体测量的鲁棒性与效率，具备实际应用价值。

Abstract: Historical astronomical plates provide a long temporal baseline for time-domain astrophysics, but degradation and structural artifacts can hinder automated astrometric calibration. We present Plate-YOLO, a framework that combines source detection and stellar classification in one detector and supplies source catalogs to a separate astrometric solver. The detector adapts YOLOv5 with Space-to-Depth feature rearrangement, SimAM attention, a high-resolution P2 head, and a Normalized Wasserstein Distance loss for small bounding boxes. A Gaia-guided fine-tuning procedure uses initially solved plates to construct labels for a new archive. On 150 UBAI plates for which the conventional pipeline had failed, the Chinese-trained model solved 40 plates, and target-archive fine-tuning recovered a further 106, giving an overall recovery rate of 146/150 (97.3%). In the reported hardware configuration, source extraction took 6.61 s per plate for Plate-YOLO and 16.73 s for SExtractor. These results demonstrate the practical value of adapting source detection to degraded historical plates.

</details>
