Motivation: 现有成像通道主要限制时空泡沫的空间涨落，而缺乏对其时间变化特性的探测手段；本文旨在发展一种基于光谱的、互补的时间域探针。

Method: 理论建模时空泡沫引起的随机到达时间抖动，分析其对一阶时间相干函数g^(1)(τ)及观测光谱的影响；推导谱线分裂（相干核+非相干基座）、宽带通量存活、谱线宽度、连续谱色散等可观测量与泡沫指数α的关系；结合GRB 221009A的MeV谱线和CMB的COBE/FIRAS数据进行约束分析。

Result: 对GRB 221009A得到α ≳ 0.63（弱约束，受杠杆臂主导）；对CMB拟合COBE/FIRAS单极谱得α ≳ 0.52（95%置信度），下一代光谱仪预计仅达α ≳ 0.54–0.55；仅TeV能段通量存活约束（α ≳ 0.74）可排除全成像窗口，但该约束依赖于近普朗克刷新时间的强泡沫模型。

Conclusion: 光谱方法为探测时空泡沫时间涨落提供了新途径；当前结果给出α的下限，均未排除全息值α=2/3，且与已有成像窗口一致；最严格的约束需依赖极端条件下的高能观测。

Abstract: Quantum-gravitational spacetime foam varying in space and in time imprints, respectively, a transverse phase decoherence and an achromatic, stochastic arrival-time jitter on light from distant sources. We show that the jitter modifies the first-order temporal coherence $g^{(1)}(τ)$ and hence the observed spectrum, providing a spectroscopic probe of the temporal variation, complementary to existing imaging-channel constraints on the spatial variation. In the dynamical-foam limit, where the foam decorrelates within the light's coherence time, the spectrum splits into a coherent line core suppressed by $e^{-σ_φ^2}$, with $σ_φ^2$ the variance of the foam-induced phase jitter, and an incoherent pedestal, yielding a spectral shape-distortion bound on the foam exponent $α$. Three probes follow: the outright survival of broadband flux, the fractional width of a spectral line, and a chromatic deficit across a broadband continuum. Applied to the MeV emission lines of GRB\,221009A we obtain $α\gtrsim 0.63$, a weak, lever-arm-dominated floor. For the broadband CMB the foam induces an $e^{-σ_φ^2(ν)}$ deficit ($σ_φ^2 \propto ν^2$); a fit to the COBE/FIRAS monopole spectrum reaches $α\gtrsim 0.52$ ($95\%$ CL), and next-generation spectrometers reach only $α\gtrsim 0.54$--$0.55$ (forecast), limited by the foam--temperature degeneracy. The pedestal-independent line-width and CMB bounds are floors below the holographic value $α= 2/3$, consistent with and excluding no part of the imaging window reported for GRB\,221009A; only the conditional TeV survival bound ($α\gtrsim0.74$), which holds only for near-Planck-refresh foam ($τ_{\rm foam}\lesssim4\times10^{-28}$\,s), would exclude it --- and, with it, the window.

</details>


### [193] [Spherically symmetric solutions with dust and radiation sources in Schouten-Codazzi Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26775)
*Sebastián Nájera,Roberto A. Sussman,Juan Carlos Hidalgo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了Schouten-Codazzi引力（SCG）理论下的精确解，聚焦于广义Vaidya和LTB类球对称时空，发现Codazzi张量引入的两个积分函数可自然产生有效宇宙学常数和额外自由度，从而在无需暗能量的情况下实现加速膨胀和永恒膨胀。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索不依赖暗能量即可解释宇宙加速膨胀的替代引力理论，Schouten-Codazzi引力作为一种含Codazzi张量的二阶度规理论，具备此潜力。

Method: 求解SCG理论中两类球对称时空（广义Vaidya和LTB）的精确解，分析Codazzi张量所满足的Codazzi方程对时空几何的约束，并推导其对Misner-Sharp质量及LTB演化方程的影响。

Result: Vaidya解中Codazzi项修正Misner-Sharp质量；LTB模型演化方程变为系数依赖Codazzi积分函数的三次多项式，支持加速膨胀与永恒膨胀解。

Conclusion: Schouten-Codazzi引力能自然产生有效宇宙学常数和额外动力学自由度，为宇宙学提供了一种无需引入暗能量的可行替代理论框架。

Abstract: We examine in this paper exact solutions of Schouten-Codazzi gravity (SCG), a second order metric theory in which a Codazzi tensor is added to the Einstein field equations. We consider two spherically symmetric geometries that describe null radiation and inhomogeneous dust, corresponding to generalized Vaidya and Lemaître-Tolman-Bondi (LTB) spacetimes. The Codazzi tensor has to comply the Codazzi equations, this imposes restrictions on the geometry of spacetime that are determined up to two integration functions that naturally generate an effective ``cosmological constant'' and an additional degree of freedom. We show that for the Vaidya geometry, these Codazzi contributions modify the Misner-Sharp mass, while the LTB models have as an evolution equation a cubic polynomial whose coefficients depend on the Codazzi integration functions. This leads to a broader class of dynamics than those of General Relativity, and allows for an accelerated expansion and ever-expanding models without the introduction of ``dark energy''. Overall, our analysis suggests that Schouten-Codazzi gravity provides an appealing framework for applications in cosmology.

</details>
